                2025
INTERIM REPORT 中期報告
The first half of 2025 saw a considerable increase in macroeconomic and geopolitical
uncertainty globally. We took swift and decisive actions to mitigate the impact from
this challenging environment. Our global reach and scale and consistent efforts to
diversify our business across brands, distribution channels and product categories
underpin our resilient business model. For the six months ended June 30, 2025, we
reported net sales of US$1,661.7 million, while our adjusted EBITDA margin was
16.2%.

2025年上半年，全球的宏觀經濟和地緣政治不確定因素均顯著增加。我們迅速採取果斷
行動，應對經營環境的挑戰。我們的業務規模遍及全球，加上推進旗下品牌、分銷渠道和
產品類別多元化方面的持續努力，是我們強韌商業模式的基礎。截至2025年6月30日止六
個月，我們呈報的銷售淨額為1,661.7百萬美元，而經調整EBITDA利潤率為16.2%。

Timothy Charles Parker
Chairman 主席

CORPORATE PROFILE
公司簡介
With a heritage dating back 115 years, Samsonite Group S.A. (together     新秀麗集團有限公司（連同其綜合附屬公司統稱為
with its consolidated subsidiaries, the “Company”, “our”, “us” or         「本公司」或「我們」）擁有115 年悠久歷史，是全球
“we”) is the world’s best-known and largest travel luggage company        最著名、規模最大的行李箱公司，並且是全球時尚
and a leader in global lifestyle bags. We own and operate a portfolio     箱包行業的翹楚。我們擁有及經營以新秀麗 、TUMI
of customer-centric and iconic brands, led by Samsonite , TUMI , and      及American Tourister 為首並以客戶為中心的標誌
American Tourister , that empower our customers’ journeys with            性品牌組合，該等品牌為我們客戶的旅程提供全球
globally trusted, innovative and increasingly sustainable products.       信賴、創新及日益可持續發展的產品。憑藉我們歷
Building on our long history of industry leadership, the Company’s        史悠久的行業領導地位，本公司的願景是引領行業
vision is to create a path toward a more sustainable future for its       邁向更可持續發展的未來。
industry.


OUR CULTURE AND STRATEGY 我們的文化及策略
The principal characteristics of our culture are:                         我們文化的主要特點是：

•      Our long-standing guiding principle, the Golden Rule, to “do       •        我們一直以來的指導原則以「待人如己」為
       unto others as you would have them do unto you,” is a legacy                黃金法則，這是我們創始人傳承下來的訓
       of our founder and guides relationships between us and our                  示，指導着我們和員工與客戶、供應商和其
       employees, customers, vendors, and other stakeholders.                      他持份者之間的關係。

•      Our decentralized management structure empowers local              •        我們的分權管理模式為當地團隊賦能，同時
       teams and fosters an entrepreneurial, innovative approach to                鼓勵合作共享最佳實踐，促進以創業且創新
       the business while encouraging collaboration to share best                  的精神經營業務。
       practices.

•      Our decentralized organizational structure encourages efficient,   •        我們的分權組織架構鼓勵高效、及時的決策
       timely decision-making and transparency, and minimizes                      和透明度，並盡量減少企業的官僚主義。
       corporate bureaucracy.

•      Our employees have a shared commitment to act lawfully,            •        我們的員工共同承諾，將按照我們的行為守
       ethically and responsibly in keeping with our code of conduct,              則合法、合道德和負責任地行事，該守則每
       which is communicated to employees throughout our Company                   年都會向全公司的員工傳達。
       annually.

•      We are focused on fostering a welcoming culture for our teams,     •        我們致力於為其包含不同國家及背景的團隊
       which represent many nationalities and backgrounds, creating                營造包容的文化，打造一個支持其僱員並有
       a workplace environment and business culture that supports                  助於彼等共同成長及發展的工作環境與企業
       our employees and helps them to grow and thrive together.                   文化。

•      Employees’ shared commitment to our Company’s “Our                 •        員工共同承諾推行我們的「負責任之旅」可
       Responsible Journey” sustainability initiatives.                            持續發展倡議。

Notes 註釋
(1) Net sales were US$1,661.7 million for the six months ended June 30, 2025, compared to US$1,768.5 million for the six months ended June
    30, 2024, a period-over-period decrease of 6.0% (-5.2% on a constant currency basis). Net sales results stated on a constant currency basis,
    a non-IFRS financial measure, are calculated by applying the average exchange rate of the period under comparison to current period local
    currency results.
    截至2025年6月30日止六個月，銷售淨額為1,661.7百萬美元，較截至2024年6月30日止六個月的1,768.5百萬美元同比減少6.0%（按不變匯率基準計
    算則減少5.2%）。按不變匯率基準計算呈列的銷售淨額業績為非IFRS財務計量工具，是以當地貨幣呈列的本期間業績採用與其相比較期間的平均匯率
    計算所得。
(2) Adjusted net income, a non-IFRS financial measure, eliminates the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-
    cash charges, along with their respective tax effects, that impact our reported profit attributable to the equity holders, which we believe helps
    to give securities analysts, investors and other interested parties a more complete understanding of our underlying financial performance.
    See “Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations — Adjusted Net Income” for a reconciliation
    from our profit attributable to the equity holders to adjusted net income.
    經調整淨收入為非IFRS財務計量工具，其撇除影響我們呈報的股權持有人應佔溢利的多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用（連同其各自的
    稅務影響）的影響，我們相信其有助證券分析員、投資者及其他利益相關方更全面了解我們的相關財務表現。有關我們股權持有人應佔溢利與經調整
    淨收入的對賬，請參閱「財務狀況及經營業績的管理層討論與分析 － 經調整淨收入」。
(3) Adjusted earnings before interest, taxes, depreciation and amortization of intangible assets (“adjusted EBITDA”), a non-IFRS financial
    measure, eliminates the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash charges. Adjusted EBITDA includes
    the lease interest and amortization expense under IFRS 16, Leases (“IFRS 16”) to account for operational rent expenses. We believe these
    measures provide additional information that is useful in gaining a more complete understanding of our operational performance and of the
    underlying trends of our business. See “Management’s Discussion and Analysis of Financial Condition and Results of Operations — Adjusted
    EBITDA” for a reconciliation from our profit for the period to adjusted EBITDA.
    未計利息、稅項、折舊及無形資產攤銷前的經調整盈利（「經調整EBITDA」）為非IFRS財務計量工具，其撇除多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現
    金費用的影響。經調整EBITDA包括IFRS第16號租賃（「IFRS第16號」）項下租賃利息及攤銷開支，以便計入經營租金開支。我們相信該等計量工具會
    提供更多資訊，有利於更全面了解我們的經營表現及業務的相關趨勢。有關我們的期內溢利與經調整EBITDA的對賬，請參閱「財務狀況及經營業績的
    管理層討論與分析 － 經調整EBITDA」。
(4) Adjusted EBITDA margin, a non-IFRS financial measure, is calculated by dividing adjusted EBITDA by net sales.
    經調整EBITDA利潤率為非IFRS財務計量工具，以經調整EBITDA除以銷售淨額計算所得。
(5) Adjusted basic and diluted earnings per share, both non-IFRS financial measures, are calculated by dividing adjusted net income by the
    weighted average number of shares used in the basic and diluted earnings per share calculations, respectively.
    經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利均為非IFRS財務計量工具，是以經調整淨收入分別除以每股基本及攤薄盈利計算所用的加權平均股份數目計算所得。
(6) Effective since the third quarter of 2024, we voluntarily made a change in accounting policy related to the recognition of the subsequent
    changes in the fair value of put option financial liabilities associated with the non-controlling interests in certain of our majority owned
    subsidiaries. The impact of adopting this change in accounting policy has been applied retrospectively and the comparative period in 2024 has
    been adjusted. All other financial statement captions for the six months ended June 30, 2024, in this table that have not been identified with
    this footnote were not impacted by this policy change. See note 2(e) Voluntary Change in Accounting Policy to the condensed consolidated
    interim financial statements for further discussion on this voluntary change in accounting policy.
    我們自願對有關確認與我們擁有大部分權益的若干附屬公司的非控股權益相關的認沽期權金融負債的公允價值的後續變動作出會計政策變動，自2024
    年第三季度起生效。採納此會計政策變動的影響已追溯應用，並已對2024年的比較期間進行調整。本表中截至2024年6月30日止六個月的所有其他財
    務報表項目（未使用本註腳加以辨識）並未受到此政策變動的影響。有關該會計政策的自願變動的進一步討論，請參閱簡明綜合中期財務報表附註2(e)
    會計政策的自願變動。

(1) Adjusted free cash flow, a non-IFRS financial measure, is defined as net cash generated from (used in) operating activities less (i) purchases
    of property, plant and equipment and software (“total capital expenditures”) and (ii) principal payments on lease liabilities (each as set forth
    on the unaudited condensed consolidated statements of cash flows).
    經調整自由現金流量（一項非IFRS財務計量工具）定義為經營活動所得（所用）現金淨額減去(i)購置物業、廠房及設備以及採購軟件費用（「資本開支總
    額」）及(ii)租賃負債的本金付款（分別如未經審計簡明綜合現金流量表所載）。
(2) Total liquidity is calculated as the sum of cash and cash equivalents per the unaudited condensed consolidated statement of financial position
    as of June 30, 2025 and the consolidated statement of financial position as of December 31, 2024 plus available capacity under the revolving
    credit facility.
    流動資金總額是按於2025年6月30日的未經審計簡明綜合財務狀況表及於2024年12月31的綜合財務狀況表內的現金及現金等價物，加上就循環信貸
    融通項下可獲得的融資額兩者總和計算。

         We delivered results in line with our expectations for the first half of 2025 despite a
         challenging macroeconomic backdrop. While we believe consumers continue to prioritize
         travel and experiences, we observed a softening in travel demand during the first half of
2025, influenced by macroeconomic uncertainty, shifting trade policies, and weakening consumer
sentiment. We expect these trends to continue in the second half of 2025, impacting near-term
demand, but we remain confident in longer-term travel tailwinds supporting the business.

儘管宏觀經濟環境充滿挑戰，我們2025年上半年的業績仍然符合我們的預期。雖然我們相信消費者仍然優
先考慮旅遊和體驗，我們觀察到2025年上半年的旅遊需求放緩，此乃由於宏觀經濟不確定因素、貿易政策
變動和消費者情緒轉弱的影響。我們預期有關趨勢在2025年下半年將會持續，從而影響短期內的需求，但
我們仍然深信旅遊趨勢長遠暢旺將為業務帶來助力。

primarily to increased retail activity during the summer travel season      由於夏季旅遊季節以及假日旅遊及送禮季節零售活
and holiday travel and gifting seasons. Towards the end of spring and       動有所增加。春末夏初，我們銷售淨額呈現上升趨

Notes 註釋
(1) The geographic location of our net sales generally reflects the country or territory from which our products were sold and does not
    necessarily indicate the country or territory in which our end customers were actually located.
    我們銷售淨額的地域位置分佈一般反映出售產品的國家或地區，並不一定為終端客戶實際所在的國家或地區。
(2) Results stated on a constant currency basis, a non-IFRS financial measure, are calculated by applying the average exchange rate of the period
    under comparison to current period local currency results. See “－Non-IFRS Financial Measures – Constant Currency Net Sales Growth.”
    按不變匯率基準計算呈列的業績為非IFRS財務計量工具，是以當地貨幣呈列的本期間業績採用與其相比較期間的平均匯率計算所得。請參閱「－ 非
    IFRS財務計量工具 － 按不變匯率基準計算的銷售淨額增長」。

Non-IFRS Financial Measures                                                非IFRS財務計量工具
In addition to our results determined in accordance with IFRS              除根據 IFRS 會計準則釐定我們的業績外，管理層
Accounting Standards, management reviews certain non-IFRS financial        亦會審視若干非 IFRS 財務計量工具，包括本節詳
measures, including constant currency net sales growth, adjusted           述的按不變匯率基準計算的銷售淨額增長、經調整
EBITDA, adjusted EBITDA margin, adjusted net income, adjusted basic        EBITDA、經調整EBITDA利潤率、經調整淨收入、
and diluted earnings per share and adjusted free cash flow as detailed     經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利以及經調整自由現金流
in this section, to evaluate our business, measure our performance,        量，以評估我們的業務、衡量我們的表現、識別影
identify trends affecting us, formulate business plans and make            響我們的趨勢、制定業務計劃及作出策略決策。
strategic decisions.

We believe that these non-IFRS financial measures, when used in            我們相信，當該等非 IFRS 財務計量工具與我們的
conjunction with our IFRS Accounting Standards financial information,      IFRS會計準則財務資料一同使用時，令投資者可將
allow investors to better evaluate our financial performance in            我們的財務表現與其他期間及我們行業內其他公司
comparison to other periods and to other companies in our industry.        對比，從而更好地評估我們的財務表現。然而，非
However, non-IFRS financial measures are not defined or recognized         IFRS財務計量工具並非根據IFRS會計準則進行定義
under IFRS Accounting Standards, are presented for supplemental            或確認，其呈列僅為補充資訊之目的，且不應被視
informational purposes only and should not be considered in isolation      為獨立於或依賴其代替根據IFRS會計準則所呈報的
or relied on as a substitute for financial information presented in        財務資料。我們呈列任何非IFRS財務計量工具，不
accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards. Our presentation of any         應被詮釋為推斷我們未來業績將不受不尋常或非經
non-IFRS financial measures should not be construed as an inference        常性項目的影響。我們所在行業內的其他公司可能
that our future results will be unaffected by unusual or nonrecurring      以不同方式計算非IFRS財務計量工具，這可能限制
items. Other companies in our industry may calculate non-IFRS              其作為可比計量工具的有用性。
financial measures differently, which may limit their usefulness as
comparative measures.

Our non-IFRS financial measures have limitations as analytical tools       我們的非 IFRS 財務計量工具作為分析工具有其局
and should not be considered in isolation or as a substitute for an        限性，不應被視為獨立於或代替我們根據 IFRS 會
analysis of our results under IFRS Accounting Standards. Constant          計準則對業績進行的分析。按不變匯率基準計算的
currency net sales growth is limited as a metric to review our financial   銷售淨額增長作為檢討我們財務業績的指標有其局
results as it does not reflect the impacts of foreign currency on          限性，原因為其不會反映外幣對所呈報銷售淨額的
reported net sales. Some of the limitations of adjusted EBITDA and         影響。經調整EBITDA及經調整EBITDA利潤率的部
adjusted EBITDA margin include not capturing certain tax payments          分限制包括不會反映可能減少我們可用現金的若干
that may reduce cash available to us; not reflecting any cash capital      稅項付款；不會反映日後可能須置換的折舊及攤銷
expenditure requirements for the assets being depreciated and              資產的任何現金資本開支需求；不會反映我們營運
amortized that may have to be replaced in the future; not reflecting       資金需要的變動或現金需求；及不會反映利息開支
changes in, or cash requirements for, our working capital needs;           或償還利息或本金付款所需的現金需求。經調整淨
and not reflecting the interest expense, or the cash requirements          收入以及經調整每股基本及攤薄盈利的部分限制包
necessary to service interest or principal payments. Some of the           括不會反映影響我們所呈報的溢利的多項成本、費
limitations of adjusted net income and adjusted basic and diluted          用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用（連同其各自的
earnings per share include not capturing the effect of a number of         稅務影響）的影響。經調整自由現金流量的部分限
costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash charges, along       制包括其不會反映我們未來合約承擔或考慮若干現
with their respective tax effects, that impact our reported profit.        金需求，如利息付款、稅項付款及債務償還需求，
Some of the limitations of adjusted free cash flow include that it does    亦不反映我們於特定時期現金結餘的增加或減少總
not reflect our future contractual commitments or consider certain         額。由於該等及其他限制，我們的非IFRS財務計量
cash requirements such as interest payments, tax payments and debt         工具應與根據IFRS會計準則編製及呈報的可比財務
service requirements and does not represent the total increase or          計量工具一併考慮。
decrease in our cash balance for a given period. Because of these
and other limitations, our non-IFRS financial measures should be
considered along with comparable financial measures prepared and
presented in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards.

Adjusted EBITDA and Adjusted EBITDA Margin                                      經調整 EBITDA及經調整 EBITDA
                                                                                利潤率
Adjusted EBITDA, a non-IFRS financial measure, eliminates the effect            經調整EBITDA（一項非IFRS財務計量工具）撇除多
of a number of costs, charges and credits and certain other non-cash            項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他非現金費用的影
charges. Adjusted EBITDA is defined as profit for the period, adjusted          響。經調整EBITDA定義為期內溢利，並經調整以
to eliminate income tax expense, finance costs (excluding interest              撇除所得稅開支、財務費用（撇除租賃負債的利息
expense on lease liabilities), finance income, depreciation, amortization       開支）、財務收入、折舊、攤銷（撇除租賃使用權資
(excluding amortization of lease right-of-use assets), share-based              產攤銷）、以股份支付的薪酬開支、減值撥回及其
compensation expense, impairment reversals and other expense.                   他開支。經調整EBITDA利潤率（一項非IFRS財務計
Adjusted EBITDA margin, a non-IFRS financial measure, is defined                量工具）定義為經調整EBITDA除以銷售淨額。我們
as adjusted EBITDA divided by net sales. We believe adjusted EBITDA             相信經調整EBITDA及經調整EBITDA利潤率會提供
and adjusted EBITDA margin provide additional information that is               更多資訊，有利於更全面了解我們的經營表現及我
useful in gaining a more complete understanding of our operational              們業務的相關趨勢。
performance and of the underlying trends of our business.

Adjusted Free Cash Flow                                                    經調整自由現金流量
We define adjusted free cash flow, a non-IFRS financial measure,           我們對經調整自由現金流量（一項非IFRS財務計量
as cash generated from operating activities, less (i) purchases of         工具）的定義為經營活動所得現金減去(i)購置物業、
property, plant and equipment and software and (ii) principal payments     廠房及設備以及採購軟件費用及 (ii) 租賃負債的本
on lease liabilities. We believe adjusted free cash flow provides          金付款。我們認為，經調整自由現金流量可有效提
helpful additional information regarding our liquidity and our ability     供有關我們流動資金及能否在不使用若干核心經營
to generate cash after excluding the use of cash from certain of our       活動之現金的情況下產生現金的更多資訊。經調整
core operating activities. Adjusted free cash flow does not represent      自由現金流量不代表可用作酌情支出的剩餘現金流
the residual cash flow available for discretionary expenditures since      量，原因為其並不包括若干強制性支出，且經調整
it excludes certain mandatory expenditures, and adjusted free cash         自由現金流量的計算可能有別於其他公司所使用類
flow may be calculated differently from, and therefore may not be          似命名的計量工具，因此未必可與之進行比較。
comparable to, similarly titled measures used by other companies.

Basis for Review Results                                                  審閱結果基礎
We conducted our reviews in accordance with auditing standards            我們根據適用於審閱中期財務資料的美利堅合眾
generally accepted in the United States of America (GAAS) applicable      國公認核數準則(GAAS)以及按照《國際審閱委聘準
to reviews of interim financial information and in accordance with        則》第2410 號由實體獨立核數師審閱中期財務資料
International Standards on Review Engagements 2410, Review of             進行審閱。審閱簡明綜合中期財務資料主要包括進
Interim Financial Information Performed by the Independent Auditor        行分析程序，並向負責財務及會計事宜之人士作出
of the Entity . A review of condensed consolidated interim financial      查詢。審閱簡明綜合中期財務資料的範圍遠較按照
information consists principally of applying analytical procedures and    GAAS及《國際審計準則》所進行之審核範圍小，而
making inquiries of persons responsible for financial and accounting      有關審核目的乃對整體財務資料發表意見，因此，
matters. A review of condensed consolidated interim financial             我們並不發表該等意見。我們須獨立於貴公司，並
information is substantially less in scope than an audit conducted in     根據與審閱有關的相關道德規定遵守其他道德責
accordance with GAAS and the International Standards on Auditing,         任。我們相信審閱程序結果為我們的結論提供合理
the objective of which is an expression of an opinion regarding the       基礎。
financial information as a whole, and accordingly, we do not express
such an opinion. We are required to be independent of the Company
and to meet our other ethical responsibilities in accordance with the
relevant ethical requirements relating to our reviews. We believe that
the results of the review procedures provide a reasonable basis for our
conclusion.

1.    Background                                                         1.    背景
      Samsonite Group S.A. (formerly known as Samsonite                        新秀麗集團有限公司（前稱新秀麗國際有限
      International S.A.), together with its consolidated subsidiaries         公司），連同其綜合附屬公司（「本公司」或
      (the “Company”, “it” or “its”), is principally engaged in the            「其」）主要在全球從事設計、製造、採購及
      design, manufacture, sourcing and distribution of luggage,               分銷行李箱、商務包及電腦包、戶外包及休
      business and computer bags, outdoor and casual bags and                  閒包以及旅遊配件，旗下品牌主要包括新秀
      travel accessories throughout the world, primarily under the             麗 、TUMI 及American Tourister 品牌以及
      Samsonite , TUMI and American Tourister brand names as                   其他自有及獲授權的品牌。本公司透過各種
      well as other owned and licensed brand names. The Company                批發分銷渠道、自營零售店及電子商貿銷售
      sells its products through a variety of wholesale distribution           其產品。本公司主要於亞洲、北美洲、歐洲
      channels, through its company-operated retail stores and                 及拉丁美洲銷售其產品。
      through e-commerce. The Company sells its products primarily
      in Asia, North America, Europe and Latin America.

      The Company’s ordinary shares are listed on the Main Board                本公司的普通股於香港聯合交易所有限公司
      of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock                   （「聯交所」）主板上市。本公司於2011年3月
      Exchange”). The Company was incorporated in Luxembourg on                 8 日於盧森堡註冊成立為一家公眾有限責任
      March 8, 2011, as a public limited liability company (a société           公司，其註冊辦事處位於13-15 avenue de
      anonyme ), whose registered office is 13-15 avenue de la                  la Liberté, L-1931Luxembourg。
      Liberté, L-1931 Luxembourg.

      The condensed consolidated interim financial statements                  簡明綜合中期財務報表於2025年8月13日經
      were authorized for issuance by the Company’s Board of                   本公司董事會（「董事會」）授權刊發，但未
      Directors (the “Board”) on August 13, 2025, and are unaudited.           經審計。本公司的核數師KPMG LLP已根據

      accordance with auditing standards generally accepted in                 認核數準則以及按照《國際審閱委聘準則》
      the United States of America applicable to reviews of interim            第2410 號由實體獨立核數師審閱中期財務
      financial information and in accordance with International               資料 進行審閱。

      consistent with those applied by the Company in the audited              的經審計綜合年度財務報表所應用者貫徹一

(b)   Changes in Accounting Policies                                     (b)   會計政策的變動
      The IASB has issued the following new, revised and amended               IASB已頒佈若干新訂、經修訂及經修改的
      IFRS Accounting Standards. For the purpose of preparing the              IFRS會計準則。就編製於2025 年6 月30 日
      condensed consolidated interim financial statements as of and            及截至該日止六個月的簡明綜合中期財務報
      for the six months ended June 30, 2025, the following standard           表而言，以下準則於本報告期間生效。
      became effective for the current reporting period.

      In August 2023, the IASB amended IAS 21, The Effects of                  於2023年8月，IASB修訂IAS第21號外匯匯
      Changes in Foreign Exchange Rates (“Amendments to IAS 21”).              率變動的影響（「IAS第21 號的修訂」）。IAS
      The Amendments to IAS 21 require companies to provide more               第 21 號的修訂規定公司在一種貨幣無法兌
      useful information in their financial statements when a currency         換成另一貨幣時，於財務報表中提供更有用
      cannot be exchanged into another currency. The Amendments                的資料。IAS第21號的修訂引入貨幣可兌換
      to IAS 21 introduce a definition of currency exchangeability             性的定義以及公司評估該可兌換性的程序。
      and the process by which a company should assess this                    此外，IAS第21號的修訂亦就公司在貨幣不
      exchangeability. In addition, the Amendments to IAS 21 provide           可兌換的情況下如何估算即期匯率提供指
      guidance on how a company should estimate a spot exchange                引，並規定公司在因貨幣不可兌換而估算即
      rate in cases where a currency is not exchangeable and require           期匯率的情況下進行額外披露。IAS第21號
      additional disclosures in cases where a company has estimated            的修訂自2025 年1 月1 日或之後開始的會計
      a spot exchange rate due to a lack of exchangeability. The               期間生效。該修訂對本公司的財務報表並無
      Amendments to IAS 21 became effective for accounting periods             重大影響。
      beginning on or after January 1, 2025. This amendment did
      not have a material impact on the financial statements of the
      Company.

(c)   New Standards and Interpretations Not Yet Adopted                  (c)   尚未採納的新準則及詮釋
      Certain new standards, amendments to standards and                       截至2025年6月30日止六個月，本公司可能
      interpretations that may be applicable to the Company are                適用的若干新準則、準則修訂及詮釋尚未生
      not yet effective for the six months ended June 30, 2025, and            效，且編製該等綜合中期財務報表時並無應
      have not been applied in preparing these consolidated interim            用。
      financial statements.

      In April 2024, the IASB issued IFRS 18, Presentation and                 於2024年4月，IASB頒發IFRS第18號財務
      Disclosure in Financial Statements (“IFRS 18”) to achieve                報表的呈列及披露（「IFRS第18號」）以令同
      comparability of the financial performance of similar entities.          類實體的財務表現能互相比較。IFRS第18
      IFRS 18, which will replace IAS 1, Presentation of Financial             號將取代IAS第1 號財務報表的呈列 ，影響
      Statements , impacts the presentation of primary financial               主要財務報表及附註的呈列，主要是規定在
      statements and notes, mainly the consolidated statements                 綜合收益表中將經營、投資及財務活動的
      of income where companies will be required to present                    收入及開支分類呈列，並規定就各個新分
      separate categories of income and expense for operating,                 類提供小計。IFRS第18 號將要求以一個單
      investing, and financing activities with prescribed subtotals            獨附註披露管理層界定的績效方法、加強
      for each new category. IFRS 18 will require disclosures about            對主要財務報表及附註之中分組（匯總及分
      management-defined performance measures in a single                      列）及信息位置的規定。IAS第1號財務報表
      note and introduces enhanced requirements on the grouping                的呈列 原本包括的部分規定移至IAS第8 號

     The Company’s net sales are subject to moderate seasonal                   本公司銷售淨額受到季節性波動的影響中
     fluctuations, due primarily to increased retail activity during the        等，主要由於夏季旅遊季節以及假日旅遊及
     summer travel season and holiday travel and gifting seasons.               送禮季節零售活動有所增加。春末夏初，本
     Towards the end of spring and the beginning of summer, the                 公司銷售淨額呈現上升趨勢，反映出為夏季
     Company’s net sales tend to increase, reflecting the purchase              假日而購買旅遊相關產品。9月至11月期間
     of travel-related products for the summer holidays. The period             通常亦為批發買家比較活躍的期間，因為彼
     from September to November typically also represents a period              等會在年底節日送禮季節來臨前增加存貨。
     of increased activity from wholesale buyers, as they increase              此外，儘管批發活動通常於 12 月降低，但
     inventories ahead of the year-end holiday gifting season.                  零售銷售通常會因年底假日相關旅遊及禮品
     Furthermore, while wholesale activity typically slows down in              購買而上升。
     December, retail sales typically increase as a result of year-end
     holiday-related travel and gift purchases.

6.   Impairment Reversals                                                  6.   減值撥回
     In accordance with IAS 36, Impairment of Assets (“IAS 36”),                根據IAS第36號資產減值（「IAS第36號」），
     the Company is required to evaluate its intangible assets with             本公司須至少每年評估無限可用年期的無形
     indefinite lives at least annually. The Company reviews the                資產。本公司審閱無限可用年期的無形資產
     carrying amounts of its intangible assets with indefinite lives to         之賬面值，以釐定是否有任何減值跡象顯示
     determine whether there is any indication of impairment below              低於其賬面值（導致減值費用），或發生事
     its carrying value (resulting in an impairment charge), or when            件或情況變化而導致無形資產（商譽除外）
     an event has occurred or circumstances change that would                   的可收回金額超過其減值賬面淨額（導致減
     result in the recoverable amount of intangible assets, excluding           值撥回）。本公司亦須於各報告期末審閱有
     goodwill, exceeding its net impaired carrying value (resulting             限可用年期的有形及無形資產的減值跡象。
     in an impairment reversal). The Company is also required to                倘有任何跡象顯示有資產可能減值或可能出
     perform a review for impairment indicators at the end of each              現減值撥回，則本公司須評估該資產或現金
     reporting period on its tangible and intangible assets with finite         產生單位（「現金產生單位」）的可收回金額。
     useful lives. If there is any indication that an asset may be
     impaired or there may be an impairment reversal, the Company
     must estimate the recoverable amount of the asset or cash
     generating unit (“CGU”).

     During the six months ended June 30, 2025, the Company                     截至2025年6月30日止六個月，本公司確定
     determined there were no triggering events that indicated that             並無觸發事件顯示其無限可用年期的無形資
     its indefinite-lived intangible assets or other assets with finite         產或有限可用年期的其他資產出現減值或需
     lives were impaired or required review for potential reversal of           要審閱是否有需要作出減值撥回。
     previous impairments.

      various forms of litigation and legal proceedings. The facts and         訴訟及法律程序。在決定未來是否較有可能
      circumstances relating to particular cases are evaluated in              出現資金外流時會評估與特定事件相關的事
      determining whether it is more likely than not that there will           實及情況，而一經確定，則評估與具體訴訟
      be a future outflow of funds and, once established, whether              相關的撥備是否足夠。本公司基於其過往經
      a provision relating to specific litigation is sufficient. The           驗及於各報告日期已知的事實及情況記錄撥
      Company records provisions based on its past experience and              備。撥備開支通常於未經審計簡明綜合收益
      on facts and circumstances known at each reporting date.                 表中的一般及行政開支中確認。當結算承擔
      The provision charge is typically recognized within general              的日期不可確切計量時，撥備將不予貼現及
      and administrative expenses in the unaudited condensed                   將被分類為流動負債。
      consolidated statements of income. When the date of the
      settlement of an obligation is not reliably measurable, the
      provisions are not discounted and are classified in current
      liabilities.

21. Financial Instruments                                                          21. 金融工具
(a)   Fair Value Versus Carrying Amounts                                           (a)   公允價值與賬面值的比較
      All financial assets and liabilities have fair values that                         所有金融資產及負債的公允價值與賬面值相
      approximate carrying amounts.                                                      若。


(b)   Fair Value of Financial Instruments                                          (b)   金融工具之公允價值
      Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an                          公允價值是於計量日市場參與者於有秩序交
      asset or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction                    易中出售資產可收取或轉讓負債須支付的價
      between market participants at the measurement date.                               格。IFRS會計準則建立一套公允價值等級
      IFRS Accounting Standards establish a fair value hierarchy                         架構，該架構排列用以計量公允價值的估值
      that prioritizes the inputs to valuation techniques used to                        方法輸入數據的優先等級。該等級架構給予
      measure fair value. The hierarchy gives the highest priority to                    於活躍市場中相同資產或負債的未經調整報
      unadjusted quoted prices in active markets for identical assets                    價最高等級（第一級別計量），以及涉及重
      or liabilities (Level 1 measurements) and the lowest priority to                   大不可觀察輸入數據的計量最低等級（第三
      measurements involving significant unobservable inputs (Level                      級別計量）。公允價值等級架構的三個級別
      3 measurements). The three levels of the fair value hierarchy                      如下：
      are as follows:

      •          Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active                 •       第一級別輸入數據為本公司有能力
                 markets for identical assets or liabilities that the                            於計量日取得的相同資產或負債於
                 Company has the ability to access at the measurement                            活躍市場的報價（未經調整）。
                 date.

      •          Level 2 inputs are inputs other than quoted prices                      •       第二級別輸入數據為不包括第一級
                 included within Level 1 that are observable for the asset                       別的報價的資產或負債的可直接或
                 or liability, either directly or indirectly.                                    間接觀察的輸入數據。

      •          Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or                 •       第三級別輸入數據為資產或負債的
                 liability.                                                                      不可觀察輸入數據。

      entirety.

our ability to protect the rights of our shareholders and enhance

Code”) contained in Appendix C1 of the Listing Rules.

Risk Management and Internal Control                                       風險管理及內部控制
The Board is responsible for ensuring that we establish and maintain       董事會負責確保我們制訂及維持適當及有效的風險

PRSUs vest in full on the third anniversary of the date of grant,                       績效掛鈎受限制股份單位僅於若干預設績效目標實

target number of shares subject to the PRSUs, if the minimum level                      的0%（倘無法達到最低績效要求）至績效掛鈎受限
of performance is not attained, to up to 200% of the target number                      制股份單位所涉及目標股份數目的200%（倘達到或
of shares subject to the PRSUs, if the level of performance is at or                    超過預先釐定的最高績效要求）。附有基於市況的
above the predetermined maximum achievement level. For any PRSUs                        績效條件授出的任何績效掛鈎受限制股份單位的開
granted with market-based performance conditions, the expense                           支於歸屬期內確認，並按授出日採用蒙特卡羅模擬

For purposes of the PRSUs granted on June 12, 2025, LTIP adjusted         就於2025年6月12日授出的績效掛鈎受限制股份單
EBITDA is defined as our consolidated earnings before interest, taxes,    位而言，LTIP經調整EBITDA定義為我們的未計利
depreciation and amortization of intangible assets, as adjusted to        息、稅項、折舊及無形資產攤銷前的綜合盈利，並
eliminate the effect of a number of costs, charges and credits and        經調整以撇除多項成本、費用及貸項以及若干其他
certain other non-cash charges. LTIP adjusted EBITDA includes the         非現金費用的影響。LTIP經調整EBITDA包括IFRS
lease interest and amortization expense under IFRS 16 to account for      第16號項下租賃利息及攤銷開支，以便計入經營租
operational rent expenses and excludes annual cash bonus expenses         金開支，但不包括年度現金花紅開支及現金長期激
and cash long-term-incentive award expenses.                              勵獎勵開支。

pay-for-performance alignment as expected by our shareholders,            圍內，實現薪酬與業績的適當掛鈎，從而形成適當
within parameters that are likely to be perceived by the grantees to be   的激勵。
achievable in order to create appropriate incentives.
